1. University of Pittsburgh Eyes on Wheels and PittMed eyeVan
Mobile. Daytime eyeVan stops and evening Eyes on Wheels clinics across Allegheny, Washington, and Greene counties, PA
🌐 https://eyeandear.org/eyes-on-wheels/
🏥 Services: Free comprehensive eye exams, vision testing, Refraction, and dilated exams, plus referrals for glasses. Separate diabetic retinopathy camera screening in primary-care clinics.
⏰ Hours: Eyes on Wheels: nights and weekends, several times a month. PittMed eyeVan: 2-3 days a week.
📞 (412) 642-5415
📧 csremoteaccess@upmc.edu

2. Penn Scheie in MOTION (OPTIC Lab mobile eye unit)
Andrew Mutch Building, Room 405, Penn Presbyterian Medical Center, 51 N. 39th Street, Philadelphia, PA 19104
🌐 https://opticlab.med.upenn.edu/mobile-unit/
🏥 Services: Free walk-up eye screening on the mobile unit: vision check, eye pressure, visual function testing, Imaging, and a telemedicine visit with an ophthalmologist. Screens for glaucoma, Cataracts, macular degeneration, and diabetic retinopathy.
📞 (215) 439-6631
📧 ScheieinMOTION@pennmedicine.upenn.edu
